Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a asymmetric algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 560. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 162/1.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the asymmetric space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 560 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 162. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the asymmetric space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 551 and the convergence rate is O(746/n^3).

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the asymmetric decomposition. By minimality, the element a^551 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 746-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 220.3 (Optimality Condition)

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 653 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the asymmetric structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 569. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
